> AGENT

The lint failure is due to `staticcheck` SA5011 errors - "possible nil pointer dereference" in `checkpoint_test.go`. The linter doesn't understand that `t.Fatal()` stops execution, so it thinks the pointer could still be nil after the nil check.

Let me look at the affected code.

> AGENT

The fix is to add `//nolint:staticcheck` comments since `t.Fatal` does terminate but staticcheck doesn't recognize it. I'll add the nolint directive to the lines flagged.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_013ws3EVzDbsNeFmdLzoouCT
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/checkpoint_test.go",
  "old_string": "\t// Verify Sessions array has 2 entries\n\tif len(summary.Sessions) != 2 {",
  "new_string": "\t// Verify Sessions array has 2 entries\n\tif len(summary.Sessions) != 2 { //nolint:staticcheck // false positive: t.Fatal above terminates"
}
```

> AGENT

The reason it doesn't trip locally is in your `.golangci.yaml`:

```yaml
issues:
  new: true
  new-from-merge-base: main
```

**Locally**: `new-from-merge-base: main` means it only checks issues in code that differs from `main`. Since these tests are already committed to your branch, and golangci-lint compares your working tree against your branch (which matches), it sees no "new" issues.

**CI**: Uses `--new-from-patch` with the actual PR diff, and explicitly sets `--new=false`. This catches any issues in the PR's changed lines, even if they were committed earlier in the branch.

The file got auto-modified (likely by `fix: true`). Let me re-apply the nolint comments.

> AGENT

All tests pass. Here's the summary:

**CI lint failure cause:**
- `staticcheck SA5011` flagging "possible nil pointer dereference" after `t.Fatal()` checks
- This is a known false positive - staticcheck doesn't recognize that `t.Fatal()` terminates execution

**Why it doesn't fail locally:**
- Your `.golangci.yaml` has `new: true` and `new-from-merge-base: main`
- Locally, golangci-lint only checks "new" issues in uncommitted changes
- CI uses `--new-from-patch` with the full PR diff, which catches issues in committed code

**Fix:**
Added `//nolint:staticcheck // SA5011 false positive: t.Fatal terminates` to 5 locations in `checkpoint_test.go`

The changes are ready to commit. Want me to commit and push them?

> AGENT

The issue is the combination of:

1. **`fix: true`** in `.golangci.yaml` - auto-fixes issues
2. **`nolintlint`** linter - validates nolint directives

`nolintlint` removes nolint comments that appear "unused" - i.e., silencing warnings that don't exist. Since local golangci-lint doesn't detect the SA5011 issue (but CI does), nolintlint sees these comments as unnecessary and deletes them.

Based on the search results, here's what I found about golangci-lint's `nolintlint` and the `allow-unused` setting:

## nolintlint `allow-unused` Configuration

The `NoLintLintSettings` struct includes the following settings:
- `RequireExplanation` 
- `RequireSpecific`
- `AllowNoExplanation` (list of linters)
- `AllowUnused` (boolean)

### Example Configuration

A typical nolintlint configuration looks like:
```yaml
nolintlint:
  allow-unused: false
  allow-leading-space: false
  allow-no-explanation:
    - errcheck
    - misspell
  require-explanation: true
  require-specific: true
```

## Known Issues

There are several known issues related to nolintlint and unused directive detection:

1. One issue reports that "when //nolint comment disables check for a disabled linter, in the example here gomnd, it is not being reported as unused, even though with the current linter configuration, it is indeed unused."

2. Another issue notes that "sometimes in our CI builds golangci-lint reports false warnings from nonolint about unused nolint:staticcheck comments. All warnings that I checked are wrong, the //nolint:staticcheck directives always silence existing deprecation warnings from staticcheck."

## General Information

golangci-lint runs linters in parallel, uses caching, supports YAML configuration, integrates with all major IDEs, and includes over a hundred linters.

The search didn't return any specific 2026-dated changes or updates to the `allow-unused` setting. The results primarily show documentation and existing GitHub issues from previous years. If you're looking for recent changes in 2026, you may want to check the golangci-lint changelog or GitHub releases directly.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

> AGENT

The `nolintlint` linter has an `allow-unused` setting. Currently your config requires all nolint directives to actually silence something - but since SA5011 doesn't trigger locally, nolintlint sees them as "unused" and `fix: true` removes them.

**Options:**

1. **Add `allow-unused: true`** to nolintlint settings - allows nolint comments that don't silence local issues
2. **Add an exclusion rule** for SA5011 in test files (if you don't want it at all)
3. **Remove `fix: true`** globally (affects all auto-fixes)

I'd recommend option 1. Want me to update `.golangci.yaml`?

```yaml
nolintlint:
  require-explanation: true
  require-specific: true
  allow-unused: true  # Allow nolint for issues that don't reproduce locally
```

Sources:
- [Golangci-lint Linters Documentation](https://golangci-lint.run/docs/linters/)
- [nolintlint false positive issue](https://github.com/golangci/golangci-lint/issues/3228)

# Staticcheck Configuration in Golangci-lint

Based on the provided documentation, I can confirm that **staticcheck is available** as a linter in golangci-lint. The documentation describes it as: "It's the set of rules from staticcheck" and notes it supports autofix functionality.

However, **the specific configuration options for staticcheck—including how to enable particular checks like SA5011—are not detailed in this page excerpt.** The documentation indicates there is a dedicated configuration page at `/docs/linters/configuration/#staticcheck`, but that detailed configuration information is not included in the content you provided.

To find the specific configuration for staticcheck checks like SA5011, you would need to:

1. Visit the full [Linter Settings](/docs/linters/configuration/) documentation page
2. Navigate to the staticcheck section specifically
3. Review the official staticcheck documentation for check codes and configuration

The page structure suggests this information exists in the documentation, but it's not accessible from the summary view shown here.

# Configuring Staticcheck in Golangci-lint

Based on the documentation provided, the page shows a comprehensive settings guide for golangci-lint linters, but the content appears to be truncated before reaching the staticcheck configuration section.

The document indicates there is a **staticcheck** entry in the linters list, but the specific configuration details are not included in the provided content. The text cuts off mid-page with "[Content truncated due to length...]"

To find staticcheck configuration options, you would need to:

1. Access the full golangci-lint documentation at `/docs/linters/configuration/`
2. Look for the `staticcheck` section, which should appear alphabetically after `sloglint` and before `tagalign`

Typically, staticcheck configuration in golangci-lint follows this general pattern:

```yaml
linters:
  settings:
    staticcheck:
      # Settings would be documented in the full configuration
```

For specific checks like SA5011, refer to the official staticcheck documentation, as golangci-lint usually allows you to enable/disable individual SA checks through staticcheck's native configuration options.
```

# SA5011 - Possible nil pointer dereference

## What is SA5011?

SA5011 detects situations where a pointer is being dereferenced unconditionally while also being checked against nil elsewhere in the code, suggesting the pointer may be nil and could cause a panic.

## When does it trigger?

This check activates when Staticcheck identifies patterns like:

- **Unconditional dereference followed by nil check**: "A pointer is being dereferenced unconditionally, while also being checked against nil in another place."

- **Common scenarios**: Improperly ordered code, missing return statements, or functions that don't abort control flow as expected (like `testing.T.Errorf`, which doesn't stop test execution).

## Is it enabled by default?

Yes, SA5011 is enabled by default. It's part of the SA5 category ("Correctness issues"), which focuses on finding assorted bugs and crashes.
```

> AGENT

Interesting. Same golangci-lint version (2.8.0) but:
- **Local**: darwin/arm64 (Apple Silicon)
- **CI**: linux/amd64

Both bundle `honnef.co/go/tools v0.6.1`, but staticcheck might have different behavior across platforms - possibly a staticcheck bug.

**Options to make local match CI:**

1. **Run in Docker** to use linux/amd64 binary
2. **Pin a specific version** and hope it's consistent
3. **Accept the difference** and add `allow-unused: true` to nolintlint

Since this appears to be platform-specific behavior in staticcheck, option 3 is probably most practical:

```yaml
nolintlint:
  require-explanation: true
  require-specific: true
  allow-unused: true  # Some checks behave differently across platforms
```

The commit I made has the correct nolint comments - they just get stripped when you run lint locally because local staticcheck doesn't flag SA5011. Should I add the `allow-unused` config so the comments survive local runs?

> DEVELOPER

arrggghh that is no good.

can we restructure the code in any way (call an explicit exit at each block?) to workaround?

> AGENT

Yes! Adding explicit `return` after `t.Fatal()` makes the control flow clear to staticcheck, even though it's unreachable.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_012nu2x1ZosNspFXYDZQEqzN
```json
{
  "command": "cat > /tmp/test_sa5011_fixed.go << 'EOF'\npackage main\n\nimport \"testing\"\n\nfunc TestNilCheck(t *testing.T) {\n    var ptr *int\n    if ptr == nil {\n        t.Fatal(\"ptr is nil\")\n        return  // unreachable but explicit\n    }\n    if *ptr == 42 {\n        t.Log(\"found 42\")\n    }\n}\nEOF\necho \"Testing if this pattern avoids SA5011...\""
}
```

title:	refactor: update checkpoint data structure
state:	OPEN
author:	gtrrz-victor
labels:	
assignees:	
reviewers:	copilot-pull-request-reviewer (Commented), cursor (Commented), entireio/cli-maintainers (Requested)
projects:	
milestone:	
number:	142
url:	https://github.com/entireio/cli/pull/142
additions:	2136
deletions:	1346
auto-merge:	disabled
--
Summary                                                                                                                                                                        
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  - Restructure checkpoint storage to use numbered session subdirectories (0/, 1/, 2/)                                                                                           
  - Add CheckpointSummary as root-level metadata with aggregated statistics and session file paths                                                                               
  - Store SessionFilePaths as absolute paths from git tree root for direct file access                                                                                           
  - Move session-specific metadata (including initial_attribution) to session subdirectories                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  Background                                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  Previously, multi-session checkpoints archived older sessions to numbered folders while keeping the latest session's files at the root. This led to inconsistent path handling 
  and made it difficult to track which files belonged to which session.                                                                                                          
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  New Storage Structure                                                                                                                                                          
                                                                                                                                                                                 
```
  <checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/                                                                                                                                       
  ├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats + session file paths)                                                                                          
  ├── 1/                    # First session                                                                                                                                      
  │   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)                                                                                 
  │   ├── full.jsonl                                                                                                                                                             
  │   ├── prompt.txt                                                                                                                                                             
  │   ├── context.md                                                                                                                                                             
  │   └── content_hash.txt                                                                                                                                                       
  ├── 2/                    # Second session                                                                                                                                     
  │   └── ...                                                                                                                                                                    
  └── 3/                    # Third session...                                
```                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  Root metadata.json (CheckpointSummary)                                                                                                                                         
                                                                                                                                                                                 
```
  {                                                                                                                                                                              
    "checkpoint_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6",                                                                                                                                             
    "strategy": "manual-commit",                                                                                                                                                 
    "checkpoints_count": 5,                                                                                                                                                      
    "files_touched": ["file1.go", "file2.go"],                                                                                                                                   
    "sessions": [                                                                                                                                                                
      {                                                                                                                                                                          
        "metadata": "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/0/metadata.json",                                                                                                                            
        "transcript": "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/0/full.jsonl",                                                                                                                             
        "context": "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/0/context.md",                                                                                                                                
        "prompt": "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/0/prompt.txt",                                                                                                                                 
        "content_hash": "/a1/b2c3d4e5f6/0/content_hash.txt"                                                                                                                      
      }                                                                                                                                                                          
    ],                                                                                                                                                                           
    "token_usage": { ... }                                                                                                                                                       
  }          
```                                                                                                                                                                    
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  Session metadata.json (CommittedMetadata)                                                                                                                                      
                                                                                                                                                                                 
  Contains all session-specific fields including initial_attribution, session_id, agent, etc.         




<!-- CURSOR_SUMMARY -->
---

> [!NOTE]
> **Medium Risk**
> Touches core checkpoint persistence/read paths and rewrites multi-session layout; risk is mainly migration/backwards-compat and any missed callers now expecting root-level transcript/prompt/context files.
> 
> **Overview**
> Refactors committed checkpoint storage on `entire/sessions` to a consistent *multi-session-first* layout: root `metadata.json` is now a `CheckpointSummary` (aggregated stats + absolute per-session file paths), and each session’s actual files live in numbered subdirectories using **0-based indexing** (`0/`, `1/`, ...).
> 
> Updates the checkpoint store API so `ReadCommitted` returns only the summary, adding `ReadSessionContent`/`ReadLatestSessionContent`/`ReadSessionContentByID` to fetch transcripts/prompts/context and session metadata. Callers across `explain`, manual/auto commit strategies, rewind/restore, and listing/cleanup logic are updated to use the new summary+session-content split, and tests/docs are revised accordingly (including moving `initial_attribution`/`summary` updates to session-level metadata).
> 
> <sup>Written by [Cursor Bugbot](https://cursor.com/dashboard?tab=bugbot) for commit b5cf65b7596ea76b8e90f4a94f4009070b056b8b. This will update automatically on new commits. Configure [here](https://cursor.com/dashboard?tab=bugbot).</sup>
<!-- /CURSOR_SUMMARY -->
```

diff --git a/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
new file mode 100755
index 00000000..3f40ace6
--- /dev/null
+++ b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
@@ -0,0 +1,266 @@
+#!/bin/bash
+set -e
+
+# Colors for output
+RED='\033[0;31m'
+GREEN='\033[0;32m'
+YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
+NC='\033[0m' # No Color
+
+SOURCE_BRANCH="entire/sessions"
+TARGET_BRANCH="entire/sessions/v1"
+
+echo -e "${GREEN}=== Checkpoint Migration Script ===${NC}"
+echo "Source: $SOURCE_BRANCH"
+echo "Target: $TARGET_BRANCH"
+echo ""
+
+# Save current branch
+ORIGINAL_BRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)
+
+# Get list of commits from source branch (oldest first, excluding initial commit)
+COMMITS=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2)
+INIT_COMMIT=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | head -1)
+
+echo -e "${YELLOW}Found commits to process:${NC}"
+git log --reverse --oneline "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2
+echo ""
+
+# Create orphan target branch from init commit
+echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
+git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
+git checkout "$INIT_COMMIT"
+git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
+git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
+git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
+
+# Process each commit
+for COMMIT in $COMMITS; do
+    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format="%s" "$COMMIT")
+    echo -e "${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}"
+
+    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree
+    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
+    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$COMMIT" 2>/dev/null
+
+    # Checkout target branch
+    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"
+
+    # Track which checkpoint directories we process
+    PROCESSED_DIRS=""
+
+    # Find all checkpoint directories (pattern: XX/YYYYYYYY/)
+    cd "$TEMP_DIR"
+    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)
+
+    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do
+        CHECKPOINT_DIR="${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}"
+        echo "  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
+
+        # Track this directory for git add later
+        PROCESSED_DIRS="$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
+
+        # Create checkpoint dir in target if not exists
+        mkdir -p "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR"
+
+        # Check if root has session files (metadata.json with session_id)
+        if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
+            ROOT_META="$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json"
+
+            # Check if this is session metadata (has session_id) or already aggregated
+            if jq -e '.session_id' "$ROOT_META" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
+                # This is session metadata at root - needs migration
+
+                # Find existing numbered subdirs
+                EXISTING_SUBDIRS=$(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*' | sort -t'/' -k3 -n -r || true)
+
+                # Calculate next session number (renumber existing + 1 for root)
+                NEXT_NUM=0
+
+                # Renumber existing subdirs (in reverse to avoid conflicts)
+                for SUBDIR in $EXISTING_SUBDIRS; do
+                    OLD_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
+                    NEW_NUM=$((OLD_NUM + 1))
+
+                    # Copy to target with new number
+                    mkdir -p "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM"
+                    # Copy non-metadata files
+                    for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
+                        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
+                            cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/"
+                        fi
+                    done
+                    # Transform metadata.json: agents to string, remove session_id and session_count
+                    if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
+                        jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
+                            "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/metadata.json"
+                    fi
+
+                    if [[ $NEW_NUM -gt $NEXT_NUM ]]; then
+                        NEXT_NUM=$NEW_NUM
+                    fi
+                done
+
+                # Move root session files to /0
+                mkdir -p "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0"
+                # Copy non-metadata files
+                for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
+                    if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" ]]; then
+                        cp "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/"
+                    fi
+                done
+                # Transform metadata.json: agents to string, remove session_id and session_count
+                if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
+                    jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
+                        "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/metadata.json"
+                fi
+
+                # Calculate total sessions (NEXT_NUM is highest 0-based index, so count = NEXT_NUM + 1)
+                TOTAL_SESSIONS=$((NEXT_NUM + 1))
+
+                # Build sessions array and aggregate data
+                SESSIONS_JSON="[]"
+                FILES_TOUCHED="[]"
+                CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=0
+                INPUT_TOKENS=0
+                CACHE_CREATION=0
+                CACHE_READ=0
+                OUTPUT_TOKENS=0
+                API_CALLS=0
+                EARLIEST_DATE=""
+
+                for i in $(seq 0 $((TOTAL_SESSIONS - 1))); do
+                    SESSION_DIR="$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i"
+                    if [[ -d "$SESSION_DIR" ]]; then
+                        SESSION_META="$SESSION_DIR/metadata.json"
+
+                        # Build session entry (paths are absolute from branch root)
+                        SESSION_ENTRY=$(jq -n \
+                            --arg meta "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/metadata.json" \
+                            --arg transcript "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/full.jsonl" \
+                            --arg context "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/context.md" \
+                            --arg hash "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/content_hash.txt" \
+                            --arg prompt "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/prompt.txt" \
+                            '{metadata: $meta, transcript: $transcript, context: $context, content_hash: $hash, prompt: $prompt}')
+
+                        SESSIONS_JSON=$(echo "$SESSIONS_JSON" | jq --argjson entry "$SESSION_ENTRY" '. + [$entry]')
+
+                        # Aggregate from session metadata
+                        if [[ -f "$SESSION_META" ]]; then
+                            # Files touched (union)
+                            SESSION_FILES=$(jq -r '.files_touched // []' "$SESSION_META")
+                            FILES_TOUCHED=$(echo "$FILES_TOUCHED" "$SESSION_FILES" | jq -s 'add | unique')
+
+                            # Checkpoints count (sum)
+                            CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=$((CHECKPOINTS_COUNT + $(jq -r '.checkpoints_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+
+                            # Token usage (sum)
+                            INPUT_TOKENS=$((INPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.input_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+                            CACHE_CREATION=$((CACHE_CREATION + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_creation_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+                            CACHE_READ=$((CACHE_READ + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_read_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+                            OUTPUT_TOKENS=$((OUTPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.output_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+                            API_CALLS=$((API_CALLS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.api_call_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
+
+                        fi
+                    fi
+                done
+
+                # Get base info from original root metadata
+                CHECKPOINT_ID=$(jq -r '.checkpoint_id // ""' "$ROOT_META")
+                STRATEGY=$(jq -r '.strategy // "manual-commit"' "$ROOT_META")
+                BRANCH=$(jq -r '.branch // ""' "$ROOT_META")
+
+                # Create aggregated metadata.json
+                jq -n \
+                    --arg checkpoint_id "$CHECKPOINT_ID" \
+                    --arg strategy "$STRATEGY" \
+                    --arg branch "$BRANCH" \
+                    --argjson checkpoints_count "$CHECKPOINTS_COUNT" \
+                    --argjson files_touched "$FILES_TOUCHED" \
+                    --argjson sessions "$SESSIONS_JSON" \
+                    --argjson input_tokens "$INPUT_TOKENS" \
+                    --argjson cache_creation "$CACHE_CREATION" \
+                    --argjson cache_read "$CACHE_READ" \
+                    --argjson output_tokens "$OUTPUT_TOKENS" \
+                    --argjson api_calls "$API_CALLS" \
+                    '{
+                        checkpoint_id: $checkpoint_id,
+                        strategy: $strategy,
+                        branch: $branch,
+                        checkpoints_count: $checkpoints_count,
+                        files_touched: $files_touched,
+                        sessions: $sessions,
+                        token_usage: {
+                            input_tokens: $input_tokens,
+                            cache_creation_tokens: $cache_creation,
+                            cache_read_tokens: $cache_read,
+                            output_tokens: $output_tokens,
+                            api_call_count: $api_calls
+                        }
+                    }' > "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
+
+                echo "    Migrated: $TOTAL_SESSIONS session(s)"
+            else
+                # Already aggregated format - copy but still transform session metadata
+                # Transform root metadata.json to have absolute paths in sessions array
+                jq --arg prefix "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR" \
+                    '.sessions = [.sessions[] | {
+                        metadata: ($prefix + "/" + (.metadata | ltrimstr("/"))),
+                        transcript: ($prefix + "/" + (.transcript | ltrimstr("/"))),
+                        context: ($prefix + "/" + (.context | ltrimstr("/"))),
+                        content_hash: ($prefix + "/" + (.content_hash | ltrimstr("/"))),
+                        prompt: ($prefix + "/" + (.prompt | ltrimstr("/")))
+                    }]' "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
+
+                # Copy and transform each session subdir's metadata.json
+                for SUBDIR in $(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*'); do
+                    SUBDIR_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
+                    mkdir -p "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM"
+
+                    # Copy non-metadata files
+                    for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
+                        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
+                            cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/"
+                        fi
+                    done
+
+                    # Transform metadata.json
+                    if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
+                        jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
+                            "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$OLDPWD/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/metadata.json"
+                    fi
+                done
+                echo "    Copied with session metadata transformed"
+            fi
+        fi
+    done
+
+    cd "$OLDPWD"
+
+    # Cleanup worktree
+    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
+
+    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed
+    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do
+        git add "$DIR"
+    done
+
+    # Commit changes
+    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
+        git commit -m "$COMMIT_MSG"
+        echo -e "  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}"
+    else
+        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}"
+    fi
+done
+
+# Return to original branch
+git checkout "$ORIGINAL_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main
+
+echo ""
+echo -e "${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}"
+echo "New branch: $TARGET_BRANCH"
+echo ""
+echo "To verify:"
+echo "  git log $TARGET_BRANCH"
+echo "  git show $TARGET_BRANCH:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json"

Now I have enough context. Let me write the review:

---

## Code Review: PR #142 - Refactor Checkpoint Data Structure

### Overview

This PR restructures the checkpoint storage format from a "latest-at-root with archived subdirectories" model to a consistent "all sessions in numbered subdirectories" model with a root-level `CheckpointSummary`. This is a significant refactoring touching core persistence/read paths.

**Key Changes:**
- Sessions now use 0-based indexing (`0/`, `1/`, `2/`) instead of keeping latest at root
- New `CheckpointSummary` type at root level with aggregated stats and session file paths
- `ReadCommitted` now returns only summary; new `ReadSessionContent` APIs for actual content
- Removed `Agents` array and `SessionCount` from `CommittedMetadata` (simplified)
- Added migration script for existing data

---

### ✅ Strengths

1. **Cleaner Architecture**: The new structure is more consistent - all sessions follow the same pattern, eliminating the special case for "latest session at root."

2. **Better Separation of Concerns**: `CheckpointSummary` (aggregated stats) vs `CommittedMetadata` (session-specific) is a cleaner separation.

3. **Comprehensive Test Coverage**: The PR adds 700+ lines of new tests including multi-session scenarios, aggregation tests, and backwards compatibility tests.

4. **Migration Script**: The `migrate-sessions.sh` script handles converting existing data to the new format.

5. **Documentation Updated**: `CLAUDE.md` is updated to reflect the new structure.

6. **CI Passing**: All lint, test, and license checks pass.

---

### ⚠️ Concerns & Suggestions

#### 1. **Backwards Compatibility Risk**
The PR removes fields from `CommittedMetadata` (`Agents`, `SessionCount`, `SessionIDs`). Any code reading old checkpoints that relied on these fields will break silently (fields will be zero-valued).

**Suggestion**: Consider keeping the fields with `omitempty` for read compatibility, or document the migration requirement clearly.

#### 2. **Migration Script Robustness**
The bash migration script is complex (266 lines) with manual JSON manipulation via `jq`. This is error-prone.

```bash
jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end'
```

**Suggestions**:
- Add a dry-run mode
- Add checksum verification before/after
- Consider writing migration in Go for better error handling
- Add rollback instructions

#### 3. **Breaking API Change**
`ReadCommitted` signature changed from returning `*ReadCommittedResult` to `*CheckpointSummary`. All callers need updating.

**Observed**: The PR does update all callers (explain.go, strategy files, tests), but this is a breaking change for any external consumers.

#### 4. **Explicit Returns After t.Fatal**
The recent commit adds `return` after `t.Fatal()` to satisfy staticcheck SA5011. This is the correct fix for the CI/local behavior mismatch.

```go
if summary == nil {
    t.Fatal("ReadCommitted() returned nil summary")
    return  // Makes control flow explicit
}
```

#### 5. **Potential Edge Case in ReadSessionContent**

```go
func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error)
```

The `sessionIndex` parameter is 0-based but there's no validation that it's non-negative. A negative index would produce unexpected path like `/-1/metadata.json`.

**Suggestion**: Add bounds checking:
```go
if sessionIndex < 0 {
    return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessionIndex must be non-negative, got %d", sessionIndex)
}
```

---

### 📋 Summary

| Aspect | Assessment |
|--------|------------|
| **Correctness** | ✅ Good - comprehensive tests pass |
| **Code Quality** | ✅ Good - cleaner architecture |
| **Test Coverage** | ✅ Excellent - 700+ new test lines |
| **Migration Path** | ⚠️ Migration script should be more robust |
| **Breaking Changes** | ⚠️ API changes - ensure all consumers updated |
| **Documentation** | ✅ Good - CLAUDE.md updated |

**Recommendation**: Approve with minor suggestions. The refactoring improves the codebase architecture. Main concerns are around migration robustness and ensuring backwards compatibility for reading old data.

Yes, looking at the migration script:

```bash
SOURCE_BRANCH="entire/sessions"

The old branch remains as a backup until you explicitly delete it or update the code to point to the new branch name.

#!/bin/bash
set -e

# Colors for output
RED='\033[0;31m'
GREEN='\033[0;32m'
YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
NC='\033[0m' # No Color

SOURCE_BRANCH="entire/sessions"
TARGET_BRANCH="entire/sessions/v1"

echo -e "${GREEN}=== Checkpoint Migration Script ===${NC}"
echo "Source: $SOURCE_BRANCH"
echo "Target: $TARGET_BRANCH"
echo ""

# Save current branch
ORIGINAL_BRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)

# Get list of commits from source branch (oldest first, excluding initial commit)
COMMITS=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2)
INIT_COMMIT=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | head -1)

echo -e "${YELLOW}Found commits to process:${NC}"
git log --reverse --oneline "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2
echo ""

# Create orphan target branch from init commit
echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
git checkout "$INIT_COMMIT"
git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"

# Process each commit
for COMMIT in $COMMITS; do
    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format="%s" "$COMMIT")
    echo -e "${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}"

    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree
    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$COMMIT" 2>/dev/null

    # Checkout target branch
    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"

    # Track which checkpoint directories we process
    PROCESSED_DIRS=""
---

{
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh",
  "content": "#!/bin/bash\nset -e\n\n#\n# migrate-sessions.sh - Migrate checkpoint data to v1 format\n#\n# USAGE:\n#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh [OPTIONS] [CHECKPOINT_ID]\n#\n# OPTIONS:\n#   -h, --help      Show this help message\n#   -n, --dry-run   Show what would be migrated without making changes\n#\n# ARGUMENTS:\n#   CHECKPOINT_ID   Optional. Migrate only this checkpoint (e.g., \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n#                   If omitted, migrates all checkpoints from entire/sessions branch.\n#\n# DESCRIPTION:\n#   Migrates checkpoint data from the old format (latest session at root, archived\n#   sessions in numbered folders 1/, 2/, etc.) to the new v1 format (all sessions\n#   in 0-indexed folders 0/, 1/, 2/, with a CheckpointSummary at the root).\n#\n#   The script reads from 'entire/sessions' and writes to 'entire/sessions/v1',\n#   leaving the original branch untouched as a backup.\n#\n# OLD FORMAT:\n#   <checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/\n#   ├── metadata.json      # Session metadata (has session_id)\n#   ├── full.jsonl         # Latest session transcript\n#   ├── prompt.txt\n#   ├── context.md\n#   └── 1/                  # Archived session\n#       └── ...\n#\n# NEW FORMAT (v1):\n#   <checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/\n#   ├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats + session paths)\n#   ├── 0/                  # First session (was at root)\n#   │   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata\n#   │   ├── full.jsonl\n#   │   └── ...\n#   └── 1/                  # Second session (was 1/)\n#       └── ...\n#\n# PREREQUISITES:\n#   - jq (JSON processor) must be installed\n#   - Clean working tree (no uncommitted changes)\n#   - The entire/sessions branch must exist\n#\n# EXAMPLES:\n#   # Migrate all checkpoints\n#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh\n#\n#   # Migrate a single checkpoint\n#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh a1b2c3d4e5f6\n#\n#   # Dry run to see what would be migrated\n#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh --dry-run\n#\n# AFTER MIGRATION:\n#   1. Verify the migration:\n#      git log entire/sessions/v1\n#      git show entire/sessions/v1:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json\n#\n#   2. To switch to the new branch (DESTRUCTIVE - backup first!):\n#      git branch -m entire/sessions entire/sessions-backup\n#      git branch -m entire/sessions/v1 entire/sessions\n#\n#   3. Push the new branch:\n#      git push origin entire/sessions/v1\n#\n# ROLLBACK:\n#   The original entire/sessions branch is not modified. If migration fails\n#   or produces incorrect results, simply delete the v1 branch:\n#      git branch -D entire/sessions/v1\n#\n\n# Colors for output\nRED='\\033[0;31m'\nGREEN='\\033[0;32m'\nYELLOW='\\033[1;33m'\nNC='\\033[0m' # No Color\n\nSOURCE_BRANCH=\"entire/sessions\"\nTARGET_BRANCH=\"entire/sessions/v1\"\n\n# Parse arguments\nDRY_RUN=false\nCHECKPOINT_FILTER=\"\"\n\nshow_help() {\n    sed -n '3,/^$/p' \"$0\" | sed 's/^# \\?//'\n    exit 0\n}\n\nwhile [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do\n    case $1 in\n        -h|--help)\n            show_help\n            ;;\n        -n|--dry-run)\n            DRY_RUN=true\n            shift\n            ;;\n        -*)\n            echo -e \"${RED}Unknown option: $1${NC}\" >&2\n            echo \"Use --help for usage information\" >&2\n            exit 1\n            ;;\n        *)\n            if [[ -z \"$CHECKPOINT_FILTER\" ]]; then\n                CHECKPOINT_FILTER=\"$1\"\n            else\n                echo -e \"${RED}Too many arguments${NC}\" >&2\n                echo \"Use --help for usage information\" >&2\n                exit 1\n            fi\n            shift\n > AGENT

Good call - dry-run by default is safer for a migration script. For the inverse:

**Options:**
- `--apply` - matches git terminology (git stash apply, git am --apply)
- `--execute` - clear intent
- `--write` - clear but less common
- `--confirm` - implies you've reviewed the dry-run first

I'd lean toward `--apply` - it's familiar from git and implies "make the changes real."

```bash
# Preview (default)
./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh

# Actually do it
./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh --apply

# Single checkpoint
./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh a1b2c3d4e5f6 --apply
```

\"${YELLOW}Found $CHECKPOINT_COUNT checkpoints on $SOURCE_BRANCH:${NC}\"\n    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do\n        CHECKPOINT_DIR=\"${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}\"\n        if [[ -f (v1)\"\ngit checkout \"$SOURCE_BRANCH\"\n\n# Process each commit\nfor COMMIT in $COMMITS; do\n    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format=\"%s\" \"$COMMIT\")\n    echo -e \"${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}\"\n\n    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree\n    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)\n    git worktree add --detach \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$COMMIT\" 2>/dev/null\n\n    # Checkout target branch\n    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)\n\n    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do\n        CHECKPOINT_DIR=\"${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}\"\n        echo \"  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n        # Track this directory for git add later\n        PROCESSED_DIRS=\"$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n        migrate_checkpoint \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$OLDPWD\"\n    done\n\n    cd \"$OLDPWD\"\n\n    # Cleanup worktree\n    git worktree remove \"$TEMP_DIR\" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf \"$TEMP_DIR\"\n\n    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed\n    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do\n        git add \"$DIR\"\n    done\n\n    # Commit changes\n    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n        git commit -m \"$COMMIT_MSG\"\n        echo -e \"  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}\"\n    else\n        echo -e \"  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}\"\n    fi\ndone\n\n# Return to original branch\ngit checkout \"$ORIGINAL_BRANCH\" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main\n\necho \"\"\necho -e \"${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}\"\necho \"New branch: $TARGET_BRANCH\"\necho \"\"\necho \"To verify:\"\necho \"  git log $TARGET_BRANCH\"\necho \"  git show $TARGET_BRANCH:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json\"\n"

   204→    # Find existing numbered subdirs

   206→    EXISTING_SUBDIRS=$(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*' | sort -t'/' -k3 -n -r || true)
   207→
   208→    # Calculate next session number (renumber existing + 1 for root)
   209→    local NEXT_NUM=0
   210→
   211→    # Renumber existing subdirs (in reverse to avoid conflicts)
   212→    for SUBDIR in $EXISTING_SUBDIRS; do
   213→        local OLD_NUM
   214→        OLD_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
   215→        local NEW_NUM=$((OLD_NUM + 1))
   216→
   217→        # Copy to target with new number
   218→        mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM"
   219→        # Copy non-metadata files
   220→        for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   221→            if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
   222→                cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/"
   223→            fi
   224→        done
   225→        # Transform metadata.json: remove session_ids and session_count, convert agents array to single agent
   226→        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
   227→            jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   228→                "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/metadata.json"
   229→        fi
   230→
   231→        if [[ $NEW_NUM -gt $NEXT_NUM ]]; then
   232→            NEXT_NUM=$NEW_NUM
   233→        fi
   234→    done
   235→
   236→    # Move root session files to /0
   237→    mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0"
   238→    # Copy non-metadata files
   239→    for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   240→        if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" ]]; then
   241→            cp "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/"
   242→        fi
   243→    done
   244→    # Transform metadata.json
   245→    if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
   246→        jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   247→            "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/metadata.json"
   248→    fi
   249→
   250→    # Calculate total sessions (NEXT_NUM is highest 0-based index, so count = NEXT_NUM + 1)
   251→    local TOTAL_SESSIONS=$((NEXT_NUM + 1))
   252→
   253→    # Build sessions array and aggregate data
   254→    local SESSIONS_JSON="[]"
   255→    local FILES_TOUCHED="[]"
   256→    local CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=0
   257→    local INPUT_TOKENS=0
   258→    local CACHE_CREATION=0
   259→    local CACHE_READ=0
   260→    local OUTPUT_TOKENS=0
   261→    local API_CALLS=0
   262→
   263→    for i in $(seq 0 $((TOTAL_SESSIONS - 1))); do
   264→        local SESSION_DIR="$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i"
   265→        if [[ -d "$SESSION_DIR" ]]; then
   266→            local SESSION_META="$SESSION_DIR/metadata.json"
   267→
   268→            # Build session entry (paths are absolute from branch root)
   269→            local SESSION_ENTRY
   270→            SESSION_ENTRY=$(jq -n \
   271→                --arg meta "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/metadata.json" \
   272→                --arg transcript "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/full.jsonl" \
   273→                --arg context "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/context.md" \
   274→                --arg hash "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/content_hash.txt" \
   275→                --arg prompt "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/prompt.txt" \
   276→                '{metadata: $meta, transcript: $transcript, context: $context, content_hash: $hash, prompt: $prompt}')
   277→
   278→            SESSIONS_JSON=$(echo "$SESSIONS_JSON" | jq --argjson entry "$SESSION_ENTRY" '. + [$entry]')
   279→
   280→            # Aggregate from session metadata
   281→            if [[ -f "$SESSION_META" ]]; then
   282→                # Files touched (union)
   283→                local SESSION_FILES
   284→                SESSION_FILES=$(jq -r '.files_touched // []' "$SESSION_META")
   285→                FILES_TOUCHED=$(echo "$FILES_TOUCHED" "$SESSION_FILES" | jq -s 'add | unique')
   286→
   287→                # Checkpoints count (sum)
   288→                CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=$((CHECKPOINTS_COUNT + $(jq -r '.checkpoints_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   289→
   290→                # Token usage (sum)
   291→                INPUT_TOKENS=$((INPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.input_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   292→                CACHE_CREATION=$((CACHE_CREATION + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_creation_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   293→                CACHE_READ=$((CACHE_READ + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_read_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   294→                OUTPUT_TOKENS=$((OUTPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.output_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   295→                API_CALLS=$((API_CALLS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.api_call_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   296→            fi
   297→        fi
   298→    done
   299→
   300→    # Get base info from original root metadata
   301→    local CHECKPOINT_ID STRATEGY BRANCH
   302→    CHECKPOINT_ID=$(jq -r '.checkpoint_id // ""' "$ROOT_META")
   303→    STRATEGY=$(jq -r '.strategy // "manual-commit"' "$ROOT_META")
   304→    BRANCH=$(jq -r '.branch // ""' "$ROOT_META")
   305→
   306→    # Create aggregated metadata.json
   307→    jq -n \
   308→        --arg checkpoint_id "$CHECKPOINT_ID" \
   309→        --arg strategy "$STRATEGY" \
   310→        --arg branch "$BRANCH" \
   311→        --argjson checkpoints_count "$CHECKPOINTS_COUNT" \
   312→        --argjson files_touched "$FILES_TOUCHED" \
   313→        --argjson sessions "$SESSIONS_JSON" \
   314→        --argjson input_tokens "$INPUT_TOKENS" \
   315→        --argjson cache_creation "$CACHE_CREATION" \
   316→        --argjson cache_read "$CACHE_READ" \
   317→        --argjson output_tokens "$OUTPUT_TOKENS" \
   318→        --argjson api_calls "$API_CALLS" \
   319→        '{
   320→            checkpoint_id: $checkpoint_id,
   321→            strategy: $strategy,
   322→            branch: $branch,
   323→            checkpoints_count: $checkpoints_count,
   324→            files_touched: $files_touched,
   325→            sessions: $sessions,
   326→            token_usage: {
   327→                input_tokens: $input_tokens,
   328→                cache_creation_tokens: $cache_creation,
   329→                cache_read_tokens: $cache_read,
   330→                output_tokens: $output_tokens,
   331→                api_call_count: $api_calls
   332→            }
   333→        }' > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
   334→
   335→    echo "    Migrated: $TOTAL_SESSIONS session(s)"
   336→}
   337→
   338→# Migrate checkpoint that's already in new format (just transform paths)
   339→migrate_new_format() {
   340→    local CHECKPOINT_DIR="$1"
   341→    local CHECKPOINT_PATH="$2"
   342→    local TARGET_DIR="$3"
   343→
   344→    mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   345→
   346→    # Transform root metadata.json to have absolute paths in sessions array
   347→    jq --arg prefix "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR" \
   348→        '.sessions = [.sessions[] | {
   349→            metadata: ($prefix + "/" + (.metadata | ltrimstr("/"))),
   350→            transcript: ($prefix + "/" + (.transcript | ltrimstr("/"))),
   351→            context: ($prefix + "/" + (.context | ltrimstr("/"))),
   352→            content_hash: ($prefix + "/" + (.content_hash | ltrimstr("/"))),
   353→            prompt: ($prefix + "/" + (.prompt | ltrimstr("/")))
   354→        }]' "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
   355→
   356→    # Copy and transform each session subdir's metadata.json
   357→    for SUBDIR in $(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*'); do
   358→        local SUBDIR_NUM
   359→        SUBDIR_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
   360→        mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM"
   361→
   362→        # Copy non-metadata files
   363→        for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   364→            if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
   365→                cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/"
   366→            fi
   367→        done
   368→
   369→        # Transform metadata.json
   370→        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
   371→            jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   372→                "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/metadata.json"
   373→        fi
   374→    done
   375→    echo "    Copied with session metadata transformed"
   376→}
   377→
   378→# Single checkpoint migration mode
   379→if [[ -n "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER" ]]; then
   380→    CHECKPOINT_PATH=$(checkpoint_to_path "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER")
   381→    echo -e "${GREEN}Migrating single checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER${NC}"
   382→    echo "  Path: $CHECKPOINT_PATH"
   383→
   384→    # Create temp dir and checkout source
   385→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   386→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null
   387→
   388→    if [[ ! -d "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH" ]]; then
   389→        git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   390→        echo -e "${RED}Error: Checkpoint $CHECKPOINT_FILTER not found on $SOURCE_BRANCH${NC}" >&2
   391→        exit 1
   392→    fi
   393→
   394→    # Show checkpoint info
   395→    if jq -e '.session_id' "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   396→        echo "  Format: old (session files at root) -> needs migration"
   397→        SESSION_COUNT=$(find "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   398→        echo "  Sessions: $((SESSION_COUNT + 1)) (1 at root + $SESSION_COUNT archived)"
   399→    else
   400→        echo "  Format: new (already has CheckpointSummary)"
   401→    fi
   402→
   403→    if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "true" ]]; then
   404→        git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   405→        echo ""
   406→        echo -e "${YELLOW}Run with --apply to perform migration${NC}"
   407→        exit 0
   408→    fi
   409→
   410→    # Ensure target branch exists
   411→    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet "refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH"; then
   412→        echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
   413→        git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   414→        git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   415→        git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
   416→        git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   417→    fi
   418→
   419→    # Checkout target branch
   420→    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   421→
   422→    # Migrate the checkpoint
   423→    migrate_checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" "$TEMP_DIR" "$(pwd)"
   424→
   425→    # Cleanup
   426→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   427→
   428→    # Commit
   429→    git add "$CHECKPOINT_PATH"
   430→    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   431→        git commit -m "Migrate checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER"
   432→        echo -e "${GREEN}Committed${NC}"
   433→    else
   434→        echo -e "${YELLOW}No changes${NC}"
   435→    fi
   436→
   437→    git checkout "$ORIGINAL_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main
   438→    echo ""
   439→    echo -e "${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}"
   440→    exit 0
   441→fi
   442→
   443→# Full migration mode - process all commits
   444→# Get list of commits from source branch (oldest first, excluding initial commit)
   445→COMMITS=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2)
   446→INIT_COMMIT=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | head -1)
   447→
   448→COMMIT_COUNT=$(echo "$COMMITS" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   449→echo -e "${YELLOW}Found $COMMIT_COUNT commits to process:${NC}"
   450→git log --reverse --oneline "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2
   451→echo ""
   452→
   453→if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "true" ]]; then
   454→    # In dry-run, show what checkpoints exist
   455→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   456→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null
   457→
   458→    cd "$TEMP_DIR"
   459→    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)
   460→    CHECKPOINT_COUNT=$(echo "$CHECKPOINT_DIRS" | grep -c . || echo 0)
   461→
   462→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Found $CHECKPOINT_COUNT checkpoints on $SOURCE_BRANCH:${NC}"
   463→    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do
   464→        CHECKPOINT_DIR="${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}"
   465→        if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
   466→            if jq -e '.session_id' "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   467→                echo "  $CHECKPOINT_DIR (old format)"
   468→            else
   469→                echo "  $CHECKPOINT_DIR (new format)"
   470→            fi
   471→        fi
   472→    done
   473→
   474→    cd "$OLDPWD"
   475→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   476→
   477→    echo ""
   478→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Run with --apply to perform migration${NC}"
   479→    exit 0
   480→fi
   481→
   482→# Create orphan target branch from init commit
   483→echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
   484→git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   485→git checkout "$INIT_COMMIT"
   486→git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   487→git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
   488→git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   489→
   490→# Process each commit
   491→for COMMIT in $COMMITS; do
   492→    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format="%s" "$COMMIT")
   493→    echo -e "${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}"
   494→
   495→    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree
   496→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   497→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$COMMIT" 2>/dev/null
   498→
   499→    # Checkout target branch
   500→    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   501→
   502→    # Track which checkpoint directories we process
   503→    PROCESSED_DIRS=""
   504→
   505→    # Find all checkpoint directories (pattern: XX/YYYYYYYY/)
   506→    cd "$TEMP_DIR"
   507→    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)
   508→
   509→    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do
   510→        CHECKPOINT_DIR="${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}"
   511→        echo "  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   512→
   513→        # Track this directory for git add later
   514→        PROCESSED_DIRS="$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   515→
   516→        migrate_checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT_DIR" "$TEMP_DIR" "$OLDPWD"
   517→    done
   518→
   519→    cd "$OLDPWD"
   520→
   521→    # Cleanup worktree
   522→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   523→
   524→    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed
   525→    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do
   526→        git add "$DIR"
   527→    done
   528→
   529→    # Commit changes
   530→    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   531→        git commit -m "$COMMIT_MSG"
   532→        echo -e "  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}"
   533→    else
   534→        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}"
   535→    fi
   536→done
   537→
   538→# Return to original branch
   539→git checkout "$ORIGINAL_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main
   540→
   541→echo ""
   542→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}"
   543→echo "New branch: $TARGET_BRANCH"
   544→echo ""
   545→echo "To verify:"
   546→echo "  git log $TARGET_BRANCH"
   547→echo "  git show $TARGET_BRANCH:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json"
   548→

> AGENT

Good question. Let me check the current behavior:

**Full migration mode**: Processes all commits chronologically. If a checkpoint was updated across multiple commits, each update is migrated as a separate commit on the target branch. History is preserved.

**Single checkpoint mode**: Currently only looks at the **latest state** of the checkpoint on the source branch and creates a single commit. It doesn't replay the checkpoint's commit history.

Is that a concern? Options:
1. **Keep as-is** - single checkpoint mode is for quick one-off migrations
2. **Add history preservation** - find all commits that touched that checkpoint and replay them
3. **Warn the user** - note in output that history is collapsed to single commit

> AGENT

The Author comes from **the git commit that created the checkpoint on `entire/sessions`**:

```go
// committed.go:1167-1214
func (s *GitStore) GetCheckpointAuthor(...) {
    // Path to the checkpoint's metadata file
    metadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName  // e.g., "a1/b2c3d4e5f6/metadata.json"
    
    // Walk commit history looking for the commit that introduced this file
    iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
        _, fileErr := tree.File(metadataPath)
        if fileErr == nil {
            // File exists - track it (oldest one with file is the creator)
            author = Author{
                Name:  c.Author.Name,
                Email: c.Author.Email,
            }
        }
    })
}
```

It finds the commit on `entire/sessions` that first introduced the `metadata.json` file for that checkpoint, then returns that commit's author info.

This will still work with the new format since the root `metadata.json` (now `CheckpointSummary`) still exists at the same path.

> AGENT

The pattern is:

```bash
git show entire/sessions:<id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/metadata.json
```

For checkpoint `7674489fb0a9`:
- First 2 chars: `76`
- Rest: `74489fb0a9`
- Path: `76/74489fb0a9/metadata.json`

This checkpoint is already in the **new v1 format** - it has a `sessions` array with 0-indexed paths. No migration needed for this one.

To see the commit that created it:
```bash
git log --oneline entire/sessions -- 76/74489fb0a9/metadata.json
```

> AGENT

Found the bug! The algorithm walks commits newest→oldest and stops at the first gap where the file doesn't exist. But merge commits inherit files from both parents, so:

1. Alex's merges (7a756c2, etc.) have the file → author = Alex
2. Victor's commits have it → author = Victor  
3. **Stefan's commits (0952571, etc.) also have it** → author = Stefan (because merges)
4. **85a43e5 (Stefan) - NO FILE** → STOP, return Stefan

The file exists in Stefan's commits because they're merge commits that inherited the file. The algorithm incorrectly attributes the checkpoint to Stefan because it stops at the first gap.

**The fix**: Use `git log --diff-filter=A` to find the commit that **added** the file, not just "has it in tree":

```bash
git log --diff-filter=A --format="%h %an" entire/sessions -- 76/74489fb0a9/metadata.json
```

> AGENT

That returns the correct author (Victor at c886ddf). The fix is to use `--diff-filter=A` in the `GetCheckpointAuthor` function instead of walking the tree looking for file existence.

```json
{
  "title": "GetCheckpointAuthor returns wrong author due to merge commits",
  "team": "Entire",
  "description": "## Bug\n\n`GetCheckpointAuthor` in `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go` returns the wrong author for checkpoints when the `entire/sessions` branch has merge commits.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe current algorithm walks commits from newest to oldest, checking if the file exists in each commit's tree. It stops when it finds a commit where the file doesn't exist (after finding commits where it does). The last author before the gap is returned.\n\nThe problem: merge commits inherit files from both parents, so a file can \"exist\" in commits that didn't create it. This causes the algorithm to attribute the checkpoint to whoever authored the merge commit before the first gap, not the actual creator.\n\n## Example\n\nCheckpoint `7674489fb0a9` was created by Victor at commit `c886ddf`, but `entire explain` shows Stefan as the author because Stefan's merge commits (0952571, 67efb66, 5d8f84f) have the file in their tree (inherited from merged branches), and the algorithm stops at 85a43e5 (Stefan, no file).\n\n## Fix\n\nUse `git log --diff-filter=A` to find the commit that **added** the file, not just any commit where it exists:\n\n```bash\ngit log --diff-filter=A --format=\"%h %an <%ae>\" entire/sessions -- 76/74489fb0a9/metadata.json\n# Returns: c886ddf Victor Gutierrez Calderon <victor@entire.io>\n```\n\nIn Go, this would use `git.LogOptions` with path filtering and check `commit.Stats()` or use the git CLI directly.\n\n## Affected Code\n\n- `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go:1167-1225` - `GetCheckpointAuthor()`\n- `cmd/entire/cli/explain.go:285` - caller",
  "priority": 3,
  "labels": [
    "bug"
  ]
}
```

diff --git a/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
index 3f40ace..0f80f09 100755
--- a/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
+++ b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
@@ -1,6 +1,86 @@
 #!/bin/bash
 set -e
 
+#
+# migrate-sessions.sh - Migrate checkpoint data to v1 format
+#
+# USAGE:
+#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh [OPTIONS] [CHECKPOINT_ID]
+#
+# OPTIONS:
+#   -h, --help      Show this help message
+#   --apply         Actually perform the migration (default is dry-run)
+#
+# ARGUMENTS:
+#   CHECKPOINT_ID   Optional. Migrate only this checkpoint (e.g., "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
+#                   If omitted, migrates all checkpoints from entire/sessions branch.
+#
+# DESCRIPTION:
+#   Migrates checkpoint data from the old format (latest session at root, archived
+#   sessions in numbered folders 1/, 2/, etc.) to the new v1 format (all sessions
+#   in 0-indexed folders 0/, 1/, 2/, with a CheckpointSummary at the root).
+#
+#   The script reads from 'entire/sessions' and writes to 'entire/sessions/v1',
+#   leaving the original branch untouched as a backup.
+#
+#   By default, runs in dry-run mode showing what would be migrated.
+#   Use --apply to actually perform the migration.
+#
+# OLD FORMAT:
+#   <checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/
+#   ├── metadata.json      # Session metadata (has session_id)
+#   ├── full.jsonl         # Latest session transcript
+#   ├── prompt.txt
+#   ├── context.md
+#   └── 1/                  # Archived session
+#       └── ...
+#
+# NEW FORMAT (v1):
+#   <checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/
+#   ├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats + session paths)
+#   ├── 0/                  # First session (was at root)
+#   │   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata
+#   │   ├── full.jsonl
+#   │   └── ...
+#   └── 1/                  # Second session (was 1/)
+#       └── ...
+#
+# PREREQUISITES:
+#   - jq (JSON processor) must be installed
+#   - Clean working tree (no uncommitted changes)
+#   - The entire/sessions branch must exist
+#
+# EXAMPLES:
+#   # Preview what would be migrated (dry-run)
+#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
+#
+#   # Migrate all checkpoints
+#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh --apply
+#
+#   # Preview a single checkpoint
+#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh a1b2c3d4e5f6
+#
+#   # Migrate a single checkpoint
+#   ./scripts/migrate-sessions.sh a1b2c3d4e5f6 --apply
+#
+# AFTER MIGRATION:
+#   1. Verify the migration:
+#      git log entire/sessions/v1
+#      git show entire/sessions/v1:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json
+#
+#   2. To switch to the new branch (DESTRUCTIVE - backup first!):
+#      git branch -m entire/sessions entire/sessions-backup
+#      git branch -m entire/sessions/v1 entire/sessions
+#
+#   3. Push the new branch:
+#      git push origin entire/sessions/v1
+#
+# ROLLBACK:
+#   The original entire/sessions branch is not modified. If migration fails
+#   or produces incorrect results, simply delete the v1 branch:
+#      git branch -D entire/sessions/v1
+#
+
 # Colors for output
 RED='\033[0;31m'
 GREEN='\033[0;32m'
@@ -10,22 +90,395 @@ NC='\033[0m' # No Color
 SOURCE_BRANCH="entire/sessions"
 TARGET_BRANCH="entire/sessions/v1"
 
+# Parse arguments
+DRY_RUN=true
+CHECKPOINT_FILTER=""
+
+show_help() {
