```
   131→    echo -e "${RED}Error: jq is required but not installed${NC}" >&2
   132→    echo "Install with: brew install jq (macOS) or apt-get install jq (Linux)" >&2
   133→    exit 1
   134→fi
   135→
   136→if ! git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree &> /dev/null; then
   137→    echo -e "${RED}Error: Not inside a git repository${NC}" >&2
   138→    exit 1
   139→fi
   140→
   141→if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet "refs/heads/$SOURCE_BRANCH"; then
   142→    echo -e "${RED}Error: Branch '$SOURCE_BRANCH' does not exist${NC}" >&2
   143→    exit 1
   144→fi
   145→
   146→if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "false" ]] && [[ -n $(git status --porcelain) ]]; then
   147→    echo -e "${RED}Error: Working tree is not clean${NC}" >&2
   148→    echo "Please commit or stash your changes first" >&2
   149→    exit 1
   150→fi
   151→
   152→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Checkpoint Migration Script ===${NC}"
   153→echo "Source: $SOURCE_BRANCH"
   154→echo "Target: $TARGET_BRANCH"
   155→if [[ -n "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER" ]]; then
   156→    echo "Filter: checkpoint $CHECKPOINT_FILTER only"
   157→fi
   158→if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "true" ]]; then
   159→    echo -e "${YELLOW}DRY RUN - no changes will be made (use --apply to migrate)${NC}"
   160→fi
   161→echo ""
   162→
   163→# Save current branch
   164→ORIGINAL_BRANCH=$(git branch --show-current)
   165→
   166→# Convert checkpoint ID to path pattern (e.g., "a1b2c3d4e5f6" -> "a1/b2c3d4e5f6")
   167→checkpoint_to_path() {
   168→    local id="$1"
   169→    echo "${id:0:2}/${id:2}"
   170→}
   171→
   172→# Migrate a single checkpoint directory
   173→# Args: $1 = checkpoint path (e.g., "a1/b2c3d4e5f6"), $2 = source dir, $3 = target dir
   174→migrate_checkpoint() {
   175→    local CHECKPOINT_DIR="$1"
   176→    local SOURCE_DIR="$2"
   177→    local TARGET_DIR="$3"
   178→    local CHECKPOINT_PATH="$SOURCE_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   179→
   180→    if [[ ! -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
   181→        echo "    Skipping: no metadata.json"
   182→        return
   183→    fi
   184→
   185→    local ROOT_META="$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json"
   186→
   187→    # Check if this is session metadata (has session_id) or already aggregated
   188→    if jq -e '.session_id' "$ROOT_META" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   189→        # This is session metadata at root - needs migration
   190→        migrate_old_format "$CHECKPOINT_DIR" "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" "$TARGET_DIR"
   191→    else
   192→        # Already aggregated format - copy but still transform session metadata
   193→        migrate_new_format "$CHECKPOINT_DIR" "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" "$TARGET_DIR"
   194→    fi
   195→}
   196→
   197→# Migrate checkpoint from old format (session files at root)
   198→migrate_old_format() {
   199→    local CHECKPOINT_DIR="$1"
   200→    local CHECKPOINT_PATH="$2"
   201→    local TARGET_DIR="$3"
   202→    local ROOT_META="$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json"
   203→
   204→    # Find existing numbered subdirs
   205→    local EXISTING_SUBDIRS
   206→    EXISTING_SUBDIRS=$(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*' | sort -t'/' -k3 -n -r || true)
   207→
   208→    # Calculate next session number (renumber existing + 1 for root)
   209→    local NEXT_NUM=0
   210→
   211→    # Renumber existing subdirs (in reverse to avoid conflicts)
   212→    for SUBDIR in $EXISTING_SUBDIRS; do
   213→        local OLD_NUM
   214→        OLD_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
   215→        local NEW_NUM=$((OLD_NUM + 1))
   216→
   217→        # Copy to target with new number
   218→        mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM"
   219→        # Copy non-metadata files
   220→        for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   221→            if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
   222→                cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/"
   223→            fi
   224→        done
   225→        # Transform metadata.json: remove session_ids and session_count, convert agents array to single agent
   226→        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
   227→            jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   228→                "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$NEW_NUM/metadata.json"
   229→        fi
   230→
   231→        if [[ $NEW_NUM -gt $NEXT_NUM ]]; then
   232→            NEXT_NUM=$NEW_NUM
   233→        fi
   234→    done
   235→
   236→    # Move root session files to /0
   237→    mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0"
   238→    # Copy non-metadata files
   239→    for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   240→        if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" ]]; then
   241→            cp "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/"
   242→        fi
   243→    done
   244→    # Transform metadata.json
   245→    if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
   246→        jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   247→            "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/0/metadata.json"
   248→    fi
   249→
   250→    # Calculate total sessions (NEXT_NUM is highest 0-based index, so count = NEXT_NUM + 1)
   251→    local TOTAL_SESSIONS=$((NEXT_NUM + 1))
   252→
   253→    # Build sessions array and aggregate data
   254→    local SESSIONS_JSON="[]"
   255→    local FILES_TOUCHED="[]"
   256→    local CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=0
   257→    local INPUT_TOKENS=0
   258→    local CACHE_CREATION=0
   259→    local CACHE_READ=0
   260→    local OUTPUT_TOKENS=0
   261→    local API_CALLS=0
   262→
   263→    for i in $(seq 0 $((TOTAL_SESSIONS - 1))); do
   264→        local SESSION_DIR="$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i"
   265→        if [[ -d "$SESSION_DIR" ]]; then
   266→            local SESSION_META="$SESSION_DIR/metadata.json"
   267→
   268→            # Build session entry (paths are absolute from branch root)
   269→            local SESSION_ENTRY
   270→            SESSION_ENTRY=$(jq -n \
   271→                --arg meta "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/metadata.json" \
   272→                --arg transcript "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/full.jsonl" \
   273→                --arg context "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/context.md" \
   274→                --arg hash "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/content_hash.txt" \
   275→                --arg prompt "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$i/prompt.txt" \
   276→                '{metadata: $meta, transcript: $transcript, context: $context, content_hash: $hash, prompt: $prompt}')
   277→
   278→            SESSIONS_JSON=$(echo "$SESSIONS_JSON" | jq --argjson entry "$SESSION_ENTRY" '. + [$entry]')
   279→
   280→            # Aggregate from session metadata
   281→            if [[ -f "$SESSION_META" ]]; then
   282→                # Files touched (union)
   283→                local SESSION_FILES
   284→                SESSION_FILES=$(jq -r '.files_touched // []' "$SESSION_META")
   285→                FILES_TOUCHED=$(echo "$FILES_TOUCHED" "$SESSION_FILES" | jq -s 'add | unique')
   286→
   287→                # Checkpoints count (sum)
   288→                CHECKPOINTS_COUNT=$((CHECKPOINTS_COUNT + $(jq -r '.checkpoints_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   289→
   290→                # Token usage (sum)
   291→                INPUT_TOKENS=$((INPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.input_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   292→                CACHE_CREATION=$((CACHE_CREATION + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_creation_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   293→                CACHE_READ=$((CACHE_READ + $(jq -r '.token_usage.cache_read_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   294→                OUTPUT_TOKENS=$((OUTPUT_TOKENS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.output_tokens // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   295→                API_CALLS=$((API_CALLS + $(jq -r '.token_usage.api_call_count // 0' "$SESSION_META")))
   296→            fi
   297→        fi
   298→    done
   299→
   300→    # Get base info from original root metadata
   301→    local CHECKPOINT_ID STRATEGY BRANCH
   302→    CHECKPOINT_ID=$(jq -r '.checkpoint_id // ""' "$ROOT_META")
   303→    STRATEGY=$(jq -r '.strategy // "manual-commit"' "$ROOT_META")
   304→    BRANCH=$(jq -r '.branch // ""' "$ROOT_META")
   305→
   306→    # Create aggregated metadata.json
   307→    jq -n \
   308→        --arg checkpoint_id "$CHECKPOINT_ID" \
   309→        --arg strategy "$STRATEGY" \
   310→        --arg branch "$BRANCH" \
   311→        --argjson checkpoints_count "$CHECKPOINTS_COUNT" \
   312→        --argjson files_touched "$FILES_TOUCHED" \
   313→        --argjson sessions "$SESSIONS_JSON" \
   314→        --argjson input_tokens "$INPUT_TOKENS" \
   315→        --argjson cache_creation "$CACHE_CREATION" \
   316→        --argjson cache_read "$CACHE_READ" \
   317→        --argjson output_tokens "$OUTPUT_TOKENS" \
   318→        --argjson api_calls "$API_CALLS" \
   319→        '{
   320→            checkpoint_id: $checkpoint_id,
   321→            strategy: $strategy,
   322→            branch: $branch,
   323→            checkpoints_count: $checkpoints_count,
   324→            files_touched: $files_touched,
   325→            sessions: $sessions,
   326→            token_usage: {
   327→                input_tokens: $input_tokens,
   328→                cache_creation_tokens: $cache_creation,
   329→                cache_read_tokens: $cache_read,
   330→                output_tokens: $output_tokens,
   331→                api_call_count: $api_calls
   332→            }
   333→        }' > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
   334→
   335→    echo "    Migrated: $TOTAL_SESSIONS session(s)"
   336→}
   337→
   338→# Migrate checkpoint that's already in new format (just transform paths)
   339→migrate_new_format() {
   340→    local CHECKPOINT_DIR="$1"
   341→    local CHECKPOINT_PATH="$2"
   342→    local TARGET_DIR="$3"
   343→
   344→    mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   345→
   346→    # Transform root metadata.json to have absolute paths in sessions array
   347→    jq --arg prefix "/$CHECKPOINT_DIR" \
   348→        '.sessions = [.sessions[] | {
   349→            metadata: ($prefix + "/" + (.metadata | ltrimstr("/"))),
   350→            transcript: ($prefix + "/" + (.transcript | ltrimstr("/"))),
   351→            context: ($prefix + "/" + (.context | ltrimstr("/"))),
   352→            content_hash: ($prefix + "/" + (.content_hash | ltrimstr("/"))),
   353→            prompt: ($prefix + "/" + (.prompt | ltrimstr("/")))
   354→        }]' "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/metadata.json"
   355→
   356→    # Copy and transform each session subdir's metadata.json
   357→    for SUBDIR in $(find "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*'); do
   358→        local SUBDIR_NUM
   359→        SUBDIR_NUM=$(basename "$SUBDIR")
   360→        mkdir -p "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM"
   361→
   362→        # Copy non-metadata files
   363→        for FILE in context.md prompt.txt content_hash.txt full.jsonl; do
   364→            if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/$FILE" ]]; then
   365→                cp "$SUBDIR/$FILE" "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/"
   366→            fi
   367→        done
   368→
   369→        # Transform metadata.json
   370→        if [[ -f "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" ]]; then
   371→            jq 'del(.session_ids, .session_count) | if .agents | type == "array" then .agents = .agents[0] else . end' \
   372→                "$SUBDIR/metadata.json" > "$TARGET_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR/$SUBDIR_NUM/metadata.json"
   373→        fi
   374→    done
   375→    echo "    Copied with session metadata transformed"
   376→}
   377→
   378→# Single checkpoint migration mode
   379→if [[ -n "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER" ]]; then
   380→    CHECKPOINT_PATH=$(checkpoint_to_path "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER")
   381→    echo -e "${GREEN}Migrating single checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER${NC}"
   382→    echo "  Path: $CHECKPOINT_PATH"
   383→
   384→    # Create temp dir and checkout source
   385→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   386→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null
   387→
   388→    if [[ ! -d "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH" ]]; then
   389→        git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   390→        echo -e "${RED}Error: Checkpoint $CHECKPOINT_FILTER not found on $SOURCE_BRANCH${NC}" >&2
   391→        exit 1
   392→    fi
   393→
   394→    # Show checkpoint info
   395→    if jq -e '.session_id' "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   396→        echo "  Format: old (session files at root) -> needs migration"
   397→        SESSION_COUNT=$(find "$TEMP_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_PATH" -maxdepth 1 -mindepth 1 -type d -name '[0-9]*' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   398→        echo "  Sessions: $((SESSION_COUNT + 1)) (1 at root + $SESSION_COUNT archived)"
   399→    else
   400→        echo "  Format: new (already has CheckpointSummary)"
   401→    fi
   402→
   403→    if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "true" ]]; then
   404→        git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   405→        echo ""
   406→        echo -e "${YELLOW}Run with --apply to perform migration${NC}"
   407→        exit 0
   408→    fi
   409→
   410→    # Ensure target branch exists
   411→    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet "refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH"; then
   412→        echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
   413→        git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   414→        git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   415→        git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
   416→        git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   417→    fi
   418→
   419→    # Checkout target branch
   420→    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   421→
   422→    # Migrate the checkpoint
   423→    migrate_checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT_PATH" "$TEMP_DIR" "$(pwd)"
   424→
   425→    # Cleanup
   426→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   427→
   428→    # Commit
   429→    git add "$CHECKPOINT_PATH"
   430→    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   431→        git commit -m "Migrate checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER"
   432→        echo -e "${GREEN}Committed${NC}"
   433→    else
   434→        echo -e "${YELLOW}No changes${NC}"
   435→    fi
   436→
   437→    git checkout "$ORIGINAL_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main
   438→    echo ""
   439→    echo -e "${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}"
   440→    exit 0
   441→fi
   442→
   443→# Full migration mode - process all commits
   444→# Get list of commits from source branch (oldest first, excluding initial commit)
   445→COMMITS=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2)
   446→INIT_COMMIT=$(git log --reverse --format="%H" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | head -1)
   447→
   448→COMMIT_COUNT=$(echo "$COMMITS" | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
   449→echo -e "${YELLOW}Found $COMMIT_COUNT commits to process:${NC}"
   450→git log --reverse --oneline "$SOURCE_BRANCH" | tail -n +2
   451→echo ""
   452→
   453→if [[ "$DRY_RUN" == "true" ]]; then
   454→    # In dry-run, show what checkpoints exist
   455→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   456→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$SOURCE_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null
   457→
   458→    cd "$TEMP_DIR"
   459→    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)
   460→    CHECKPOINT_COUNT=$(echo "$CHECKPOINT_DIRS" | grep -c . || echo 0)
   461→
   462→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Found $CHECKPOINT_COUNT checkpoints on $SOURCE_BRANCH:${NC}"
   463→    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do
   464→        CHECKPOINT_DIR="${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}"
   465→        if [[ -f "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" ]]; then
   466→            if jq -e '.session_id' "$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
   467→                echo "  $CHECKPOINT_DIR (old format)"
   468→            else
   469→                echo "  $CHECKPOINT_DIR (new format)"
   470→            fi
   471→        fi
   472→    done
   473→
   474→    cd "$OLDPWD"
   475→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   476→
   477→    echo ""
   478→    echo -e "${YELLOW}Run with --apply to perform migration${NC}"
   479→    exit 0
   480→fi
   481→
   482→# Create orphan target branch from init commit
   483→echo -e "${GREEN}Creating target branch $TARGET_BRANCH...${NC}"
   484→git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   485→git checkout "$INIT_COMMIT"
   486→git checkout --orphan "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   487→git commit --allow-empty -m "Initialize metadata branch (v1)"
   488→git checkout "$SOURCE_BRANCH"
   489→
   490→# Process each commit
   491→for COMMIT in $COMMITS; do
   492→    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format="%s" "$COMMIT")
   493→    echo -e "${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}"
   494→
   495→    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree
   496→    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)
   497→    git worktree add --detach "$TEMP_DIR" "$COMMIT" 2>/dev/null
   498→
   499→    # Checkout target branch
   500→    git checkout "$TARGET_BRANCH"
   501→
   502→    # Track which checkpoint directories we process
   503→    PROCESSED_DIRS=""
   504→
   505→    # Find all checkpoint directories (pattern: XX/YYYYYYYY/)
   506→    cd "$TEMP_DIR"
   507→    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)
   508→
   509→    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do
   510→        CHECKPOINT_DIR="${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}"
   511→        echo "  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   512→
   513→        # Track this directory for git add later
   514→        PROCESSED_DIRS="$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR"
   515→
   516→        migrate_checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT_DIR" "$TEMP_DIR" "$OLDPWD"
   517→    done
   518→
   519→    cd "$OLDPWD"
   520→
   521→    # Cleanup worktree
   522→    git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
   523→
   524→    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed
   525→    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do
   526→        git add "$DIR"
   527→    done
   528→
   529→    # Commit changes
   530→    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   531→        git commit -m "$COMMIT_MSG"
   532→        echo -e "  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}"
   533→    else
   534→        echo -e "  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}"
   535→    fi
   536→done
   537→
   538→# Return to original branch
   539→git checkout "$ORIGINAL_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || git checkout main
   540→
   541→echo ""
   542→echo -e "${GREEN}=== Migration Complete ===${NC}"
   543→echo "New branch: $TARGET_BRANCH"
   544→echo ""
   545→echo "To verify:"
   546→echo "  git log $TARGET_BRANCH"
   547→echo "  git show $TARGET_BRANCH:<checkpoint_path>/metadata.json"
   548→
```

> AGENT

Yes, but with caveats. Looking at the detection logic in `migrate_checkpoint()` (lines 186-194):

```bash
if jq -e '.session_id' "$ROOT_META" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
    # Old format - needs migration
    migrate_old_format ...
else
    # Already aggregated format
    migrate_new_format ...
fi
```

It detects v1 checkpoints (root `metadata.json` has no `session_id` field) and routes them to `migrate_new_format()`. However, that function still applies transformations:
- Converts session paths to absolute format
- Removes `session_ids`/`session_count` from session metadata
- Converts `agents` array to single agent

So it's not a pure pass-through. If a checkpoint is **already fully v1 compliant**, these transformations are redundant but shouldn't break anything.

**Potential issue:** If you run migration twice, the full migration mode will fail trying to create `entire/sessions/v1` branch that already exists. The single-checkpoint mode handles this better (checks if target branch exists first).

Want me to add idempotency support - skip checkpoints that already exist on the target branch unchanged?

> DEVELOPER

I'll add idempotency support to skip checkpoints that already exist on the target branch.

```json
  "old_string": "# Convert checkpoint ID to path pattern (e.g., \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\" -> \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\")\ncheckpoint_to_path() {\n    local id=\"$1\"\n    echo \"${id:0:2}/${id:2}\"\n}",
  "new_string": "# Convert checkpoint ID to path pattern (e.g., \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\" -> \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\")\ncheckpoint_to_path() {\n    local id=\"$1\"\n    echo \"${id:0:2}/${id:2}\"\n}\n\n# Check if a checkpoint already exists on target branch and is in v1 format\n# Returns 0 if exists and valid, 1 otherwise\ncheckpoint_exists_on_target() {\n    local checkpoint_path=\"$1\"\n\n    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet \"refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if metadata.json exists on target\n    if ! git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" &>/dev/null; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if it has sessions array (v1 format indicator)\n    if git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" | jq -e '.sessions' &>/dev/null; then\n        return 0\n    fi\n\n    return 1\n}"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "# Migrate a single checkpoint directory\n# Args: $1 = checkpoint path (e.g., \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\"), $2 = source dir, $3 = target dir\nmigrate_checkpoint() {\n    local CHECKPOINT_DIR=\"$1\"\n    local SOURCE_DIR=\"$2\"\n    local TARGET_DIR=\"$3\"\n    local CHECKPOINT_PATH=\"$SOURCE_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n    if [[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json\" ]]; then\n        echo \"    Skipping: no metadata.json\"\n        return\n    fi\n\n    local ROOT_META=\"$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json\"\n\n    # Check if this is session metadata (has session_id) or already aggregated\n    if jq -e '.session_id' \"$ROOT_META\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n        # This is session metadata at root - needs migration\n        migrate_old_format \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH\" \"$TARGET_DIR\"\n    else\n        # Already aggregated format - copy but still transform session metadata\n        migrate_new_format \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH\" \"$TARGET_DIR\"\n    fi\n}",
  "new_string": "# Migrate a single checkpoint directory\n# Args: $1 = checkpoint path (e.g., \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\"), $2 = source dir, $3 = target dir\n# Returns: 0 if migrated, 1 if skipped\nmigrate_checkpoint() {\n    local CHECKPOINT_DIR=\"$1\"\n    local SOURCE_DIR=\"$2\"\n    local TARGET_DIR=\"$3\"\n    local CHECKPOINT_PATH=\"$SOURCE_DIR/$CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n    if [[ ! -f \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json\" ]]; then\n        echo \"    Skipping: no metadata.json\"\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if already migrated to target branch\n    if checkpoint_exists_on_target \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\"; then\n        echo \"    Skipping: already exists on $TARGET_BRANCH\"\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    local ROOT_META=\"$CHECKPOINT_PATH/metadata.json\"\n\n    # Check if this is session metadata (has session_id) or already aggregated\n    if jq -e '.session_id' \"$ROOT_META\" > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n        # This is session metadata at root - needs migration\n        migrate_old_format \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH\" \"$TARGET_DIR\"\n    else\n        # Already aggregated format - copy but still transform session metadata\n        migrate_new_format \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$CHECKPOINT_PATH\" \"$TARGET_DIR\"\n    fi\n    return 0\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Added idempotency support with these changes:

1. **`checkpoint_exists_on_target()`** - New function that checks if a checkpoint already exists on the target branch with a valid v1 `sessions` array

2. **`migrate_checkpoint()`** - Now checks for existing checkpoints and skips them with a message

3. **Single-checkpoint mode** - Exits early with "Already migrated" message if checkpoint exists

4. **Full migration mode** - Uses existing target branch if present (instead of failing)

5. **Dry-run output** - Shows which checkpoints are already migrated vs need migration

6. **Documentation** - Added note about idempotency

The script can now be run repeatedly - it will only migrate new checkpoints and skip ones already on `entire/sessions/v1`.

> DEVELOPER

how do I ensure I have the latest entire/sessions from remote?

> AGENT

```bash
git fetch origin entire/sessions:entire/sessions
```

This fetches the remote branch and updates (or creates) the local branch to match. If you already have a local `entire/sessions` with changes, you can force it:

```bash
git fetch origin entire/sessions:entire/sessions --force
```

Or the two-step approach:

```bash
git fetch origin
git checkout entire/sessions && git pull origin entire/sessions
```

> DEVELOPER

how do I push my local entire/sessions without checking it out?

> AGENT

```bash
git push origin entire/sessions:entire/sessions
```

Or simply:

```bash
git push origin entire/sessions
```

I see the issue. We should check before creating worktrees. Let me fix the loop:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Cwexx2JbsmWPNiHwS87o8P
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh",
  "old_string": "# Process each commit\nfor COMMIT in $COMMITS; do\n    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format=\"%s\" \"$COMMIT\")\n    echo -e \"${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}\"\n\n    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree\n    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)\n    git worktree add --detach \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$COMMIT\" 2>/dev/null\n\n    # Checkout target branch\n    git checkout \"$TARGET_BRANCH\"\n\n    # Track which checkpoint directories we process\n    PROCESSED_DIRS=\"\"\n\n    # Find all checkpoint directories (pattern: XX/YYYYYYYY/)\n    cd \"$TEMP_DIR\"\n    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(find . -maxdepth 2 -mindepth 2 -type d | grep -E '^\\./[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)\n\n    for CHECKPOINT_PATH in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do\n        CHECKPOINT_DIR=\"${CHECKPOINT_PATH#./}\"\n        echo \"  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n        # Track this directory for git add later\n        PROCESSED_DIRS=\"$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n        migrate_checkpoint \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$OLDPWD\"\n    done\n\n    cd \"$OLDPWD\"\n\n    # Cleanup worktree\n    git worktree remove \"$TEMP_DIR\" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf \"$TEMP_DIR\"\n\n    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed\n    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do\n        git add \"$DIR\"\n    done\n\n    # Commit changes\n    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n        git commit -m \"$COMMIT_MSG\"\n        echo -e \"  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}\"\n    else\n        echo -e \"  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}\"\n    fi\ndone",
  "new_string": "# Process each commit\nfor COMMIT in $COMMITS; do\n    COMMIT_MSG=$(git log -1 --format=\"%s\" \"$COMMIT\")\n\n    # Find checkpoint directories in this commit (without creating worktree)\n    CHECKPOINT_DIRS=$(git ls-tree -d --name-only -r \"$COMMIT\" | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+$' || true)\n\n    if [[ -z \"$CHECKPOINT_DIRS\" ]]; then\n        echo -e \"${YELLOW}Skipping commit (no checkpoints): $COMMIT_MSG${NC}\"\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    # Check if any checkpoints need migration\n    NEEDS_MIGRATION=false\n    for CHECKPOINT_DIR in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do\n        if ! checkpoint_exists_on_target \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\"; then\n            NEEDS_MIGRATION=true\n            break\n        fi\n    done\n\n    if [[ \"$NEEDS_MIGRATION\" == \"false\" ]]; then\n        echo -e \"${YELLOW}Skipping commit (all migrated): $COMMIT_MSG${NC}\"\n        continue\n    fi\n\n    echo -e \"${GREEN}Processing commit: $COMMIT_MSG${NC}\"\n\n    # Checkout source commit in temp worktree\n    TEMP_DIR=$(mktemp -d)\n    git worktree add --detach \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$COMMIT\" 2>/dev/null\n\n    # Checkout target branch\n    git checkout \"$TARGET_BRANCH\"\n\n    # Track which checkpoint directories we process\n    PROCESSED_DIRS=\"\"\n\n    # Process checkpoints\n    for CHECKPOINT_DIR in $CHECKPOINT_DIRS; do\n        echo \"  Processing checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n\n        if migrate_checkpoint \"$CHECKPOINT_DIR\" \"$TEMP_DIR\" \"$(pwd)\"; then\n            # Track this directory for git add later\n            PROCESSED_DIRS=\"$PROCESSED_DIRS $CHECKPOINT_DIR\"\n        fi\n    done\n\n    # Cleanup worktree\n    git worktree remove \"$TEMP_DIR\" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf \"$TEMP_DIR\"\n\n    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed\n    for DIR in $PROCESSED_DIRS; do\n        git add \"$DIR\"\n    done\n\n    # Commit changes\n    if ! git diff --cached --quiet; then\n        git commit -m \"$COMMIT_MSG\"\n        echo -e \"  ${GREEN}Committed${NC}\"\n    else\n        echo -e \"  ${YELLOW}No changes${NC}\"\n    fi\ndone"
}
```

> AGENT

The current code wraps `migrate_checkpoint` in an `if` statement:

```bash
if migrate_checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT_DIR" "$TEMP_DIR" "$(pwd)"; then
```

Commands in `if` conditionals are exempt from `set -e`, so when `migrate_checkpoint` returns 1 (skip), it won't exit the script - the `if` block just doesn't execute and the loop continues naturally.

That said, we're now checking twice - once before creating the worktree (to skip the whole commit) and again inside `migrate_checkpoint`. The inner check is redundant. Want me to remove it from `migrate_checkpoint` since we're pre-filtering?

> AGENT

Summary of changes:
1. **Removed redundant check** from `migrate_checkpoint` (pre-filtering handles it)
2. **Skip commits early** using `git ls-tree` before creating worktrees
3. **Simplified processing loop** - no more `cd` back and forth

Ready to commit when you are.

> DEVELOPER

ummm, so I guess this skip-check doesn't handle the 'update' commit case?

> AGENT

You're right - if a checkpoint gets updated in a later commit (e.g., more sessions added), we'd skip it because it already exists on target, even though the target has stale data.

Options:
1. **Compare content** - check if source differs from target (e.g., session count, content hash)
2. **Track source commits** - store which source commit was migrated, only skip if same commit
3. **Always re-migrate** - remove idempotency, always overwrite (simpler but slower)
4. **Timestamp-based** - compare last modified times

What's the expected use case? Is updating existing checkpoints common, or is this mainly for incremental migration of new checkpoints?

> DEVELOPER

okay, let's do this:
1. let's do the source commit tracking
2. can we retain the original author for the commit in the new branch?

> AGENT

Good ideas. Here's the plan:

1. **Source commit tracking**: Add `source_commit` field to the checkpoint's `metadata.json` on target. When checking, compare source commits - skip only if same.

2. **Preserve author**: Extract author from source commit and use `git commit --author="..."` on target.

Let me implement both:

id: toolu_01Ny4at5XQYngt9NDdAofmE6
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh",
  "old_string": "# Check if a checkpoint already exists on target branch and is in v1 format\n# Returns 0 if exists and valid, 1 otherwise\ncheckpoint_exists_on_target() {\n    local checkpoint_path=\"$1\"\n\n    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet \"refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if metadata.json exists on target\n    if ! git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" &>/dev/null; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if it has sessions array (v1 format indicator)\n    if git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" | jq -e '.sessions' &>/dev/null; then\n        return 0\n    fi\n\n    return 1\n}",
  "new_string": "# Check if a checkpoint already exists on target branch from the same source commit\n# Args: $1 = checkpoint path, $2 = source commit hash\n# Returns 0 if exists and up-to-date, 1 otherwise\ncheckpoint_up_to_date_on_target() {\n    local checkpoint_path=\"$1\"\n    local source_commit=\"$2\"\n\n    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet \"refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if metadata.json exists on target\n    if ! git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" &>/dev/null; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if it has sessions array (v1 format indicator) and same source commit\n    local target_source_commit\n    target_source_commit=$(git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.source_commit // \"\"')\n    \n    if [[ \"$target_source_commit\" == \"$source_commit\" ]]; then\n        return 0\n    fi\n\n    return 1\n}"
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh",
  "old_string": "# Check if a checkpoint already exists on target branch and is in v1 format\n# Returns 0 if exists and valid, 1 otherwise\ncheckpoint_exists_on_target() {\n    local checkpoint_path=\"$1\"\n\n    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet \"refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if metadata.json exists on target\n    if ! git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" &>/dev/null; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if it has sessions array (v1 format indicator)\n    if git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" | jq -e '.sessions' &>/dev/null; then\n        return 0\n    fi\n\n    return 1\n}",
  "new_string": "# Check if a checkpoint is up-to-date on target branch (same source commit)\n# Args: $1 = checkpoint path, $2 = source commit hash\n# Returns 0 if exists and up-to-date, 1 otherwise\ncheckpoint_up_to_date_on_target() {\n    local checkpoint_path=\"$1\"\n    local source_commit=\"$2\"\n\n    if ! git show-ref --verify --quiet \"refs/heads/$TARGET_BRANCH\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if metadata.json exists on target\n    if ! git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" &>/dev/null; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    # Check if same source commit (up-to-date)\n    local target_source_commit\n    target_source_commit=$(git show \"$TARGET_BRANCH:$checkpoint_path/metadata.json\" 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.migration_source_commit // \"\"')\n\n    if [[ -n \"$target_source_commit\" && \"$target_source_commit\" == \"$source_commit\" ]]; then\n        return 0\n    fi\n\n    return 1\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. Summary of changes:

1. **Source commit tracking** - Added `migration_source_commit` field to migrated metadata
2. **Up-to-date check** - `checkpoint_up_to_date_on_target()` compares source commits, not just existence
3. **Author preservation** - Both single and full migration use `git commit --author="$COMMIT_AUTHOR"`
4. **Updated all call sites** - Source commit passed through the entire chain

Now if a checkpoint is updated in a later commit, the migration will detect the different source commit and re-migrate it.

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/4/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh",
  "old_string": "# Convert checkpoint ID to path pattern (e.g., \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\" -> \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\")\ncheckpoint_to_path() {\n    local id=\"$1\"\n    echo \"${id:0:2}/${id:2}\"\n}\n\n# Check if a checkpoint is up-to-date on target branch (same source commit)",
  "new_string": "# Convert checkpoint ID to path pattern (e.g., \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\" -> \"a1/b2c3d4e5f6\")\ncheckpoint_to_path() {\n    local id=\"$1\"\n    echo \"${id:0:2}/${id:2}\"\n}\n\n# Retry a git command with exponential backoff (handles index.lock race conditions)\n# Args: $@ = command to run\ngit_retry() {\n    local max_attempts=5\n    local attempt=1\n    local wait_time=0.2\n\n    while [[ $attempt -le $max_attempts ]]; do\n        if \"$@\" 2>&1; then\n            return 0\n        fi\n\n        if [[ $attempt -lt $max_attempts ]]; then\n            echo \"    Retrying in ${wait_time}s (attempt $attempt/$max_attempts)...\" >&2\n            sleep \"$wait_time\"\n            wait_time=$(echo \"$wait_time * 2\" | bc)\n        fi\n        ((attempt++))\n    done\n\n    echo \"    Failed after $max_attempts attempts\" >&2\n    return 1\n}\n\n# Check if a checkpoint is up-to-date on target branch (same source commit)"
}
```

```
diff --git a/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
index 4432d02..7090daf 100755
--- a/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
+++ b/scripts/migrate-sessions.sh
@@ -173,6 +173,30 @@ checkpoint_to_path() {
     echo "${id:0:2}/${id:2}"
 }
 
+# Retry a git command with exponential backoff (handles index.lock race conditions)
+# Args: $@ = command to run
+git_retry() {
+    local max_attempts=5
+    local attempt=1
+    local wait_time=0.2
+
+    while [[ $attempt -le $max_attempts ]]; do
+        if "$@" 2>&1; then
+            return 0
+        fi
+
+        if [[ $attempt -lt $max_attempts ]]; then
+            echo "    Retrying in ${wait_time}s (attempt $attempt/$max_attempts)..." >&2
+            sleep "$wait_time"
+            wait_time=$(echo "$wait_time * 2" | bc)
+        fi
+        ((attempt++))
+    done
+
+    echo "    Failed after $max_attempts attempts" >&2
+    return 1
+}
+
 # Check if a checkpoint is up-to-date on target branch (same source commit)
 # Args: $1 = checkpoint path, $2 = source commit hash
 # Returns 0 if exists and up-to-date, 1 otherwise
@@ -479,10 +503,10 @@ if [[ -n "$CHECKPOINT_FILTER" ]]; then
     # Cleanup
     git worktree remove "$TEMP_DIR" --force 2>/dev/null || rm -rf "$TEMP_DIR"
 
-    # Commit with original author
-    git add "$CHECKPOINT_PATH"
+    # Commit with original author (with retry for lock issues)

-        git commit --author="$COMMIT_AUTHOR" -m "Migrate checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER"
+        git_retry git commit --author="$COMMIT_AUTHOR" -m "Migrate checkpoint: $CHECKPOINT_FILTER"

@@ -609,14 +633,14 @@ for COMMIT in $COMMITS; do

+    # Only add the specific checkpoint directories we processed (with retry for lock issues)

-    # Commit changes with original author
```

> DEVELOPER

Q: are we looping through every single checkpoint on every single commit? 🤔

> AGENT

Yes, that's inefficient. Currently for each commit, `git ls-tree` returns **all** checkpoints in the tree at that point, not just the ones changed in that commit.

Better approach: use `git diff-tree` to find only checkpoints **modified in each commit**:

```bash
git diff-tree --no-commit-id --name-only -r "$COMMIT" | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]{2}/[0-9a-f]+/' | cut -d'/' -f1-2 | sort -u
```

This returns only the checkpoint directories that were actually touched in that specific commit. Want me to fix this?

> DEVELOPER

yes please
